and various business sectors of the Group. At the same time, the real estate       與尋底階段，特別是三四線及縣域市場，房企
market in Mainland remained in a phase of deep adjustment and bottoming            去庫存壓力沉重、銷售修復緩慢的長尾效應，
out, particularly in third- and fourth-tier city-level and county-level markets.   進一步壓抑了整體市場氛圍與居民消費信心。

developers and the slow recovery in sales have further dampened overall            持推動產業轉型和創新發展經營方針，善用數
market sentiment and consumer confidence. Amid an unclear macro-                   字化、智能化及低碳化轉型給產業帶來的各種

by its proprietary technology. At the same time, keeping pace with the             團精準研判前瞻趨勢，並且積極響應實現碳達

During the reporting period, the domestic household appliances market                在本報告期間，國內家電市場處於持續的紅海
remained mired in intense competition. Coupled with the long-tail effects of a       競爭，疊加房地產市場萎縮的長尾效應，整體
shrinking real estate sector, the overall business environment continues to          業務環境仍面臨挑戰。雖然國家「以舊換新」政
face challenges. Although the household appliances business was still                策仍然為家電業務提供支撐，本集團仍採取積
supported by the national “trade-in” policy, the Group actively pursued an           極進取的海外擴張戰略並於 2026 年上半年初見
overseas expansion strategy that began to yield results in the first half of 2026,   成效，整體營收得以錄得增長及有助分散單一
leading to an increase in overall revenue and mitigating single-market risks.        市場風險。受惠於半導體行業市場估值於本期
Benefiting from a significant rebound in market valuations within the                末的大幅反彈，本集團現代服務業業務板塊中
semiconductor industry at the end of the Current Period, the fair value of           創投業務持有的部份上市及非上市股權證券公
certain listed and unlisted equity securities held by the Group’s venture capital    允價值大幅上升，致使錄得顯著的公允價值變
business — part of its modern services business sector — rose substantially,         動收益；該項收益屬未變現之非現金帳面調整，
resulting in a significant gain from changes in fair value; this gain constitutes    並不影響本集團本期之實際現金流。本集團於
an unrealised, non-cash accounting adjustment and does not affect the                期內密切關注市場發展及採取穩健及靈活的營
Group’s actual cash flows for the Current Period. During the Current Period, the     運策略，包括優化資源配置及加快調整庫存結
Group closely monitored market developments and adopted prudent and                  構去應對激烈競爭環境，並以優質的產品質量
flexible operational strategies, including optimising resource allocation and        打造強而有力的品牌競爭力，經營狀況及財務
accelerating inventory structure adjustments, to cope with intense                   表現保持穩定，各項工作有序推進。
competition. By leveraging high-quality products to strengthen brand
competitiveness, the Group maintained stable operating conditions and
financial performance, with all key initiatives progressing in an orderly manner.

TREASURY POLICY                                                                     財資政策
The Group’s major investments and revenue streams are derived from                  本集團大部分投資及收入來源於中國大陸，主
Mainland China. The Group’s assets and liabilities are mainly denominated in        要資產及負債以人民幣結算，其餘部分則以港
RMB, others are denominated in Hong Kong dollars, US dollars and Euros. The         元、美元及歐元等外幣結算。本集團主要透過
Group uses general trade financing to fulfil the needs in operating cash flow. In   一般貿易融資方式滿足營運現金需求，並積極
order to reduce finance costs, the Group exploits the currency-based and            運用銀行提供的貨幣理財產品及收益型理財工
income-based financial management tools introduced by banks to offset such          具，有效平衡融資成本。
costs.

Since the first half of 2026, the expected U.S. Federal Reserve rate cuts have      自 2026 年上半年以來，美聯儲降息預期多次反
fluctuated repeatedly, whilst market discussions regarding the persistence of       覆，市場對通脹韌性及潛在加息路徑的討論顯
inflation and the potential path of interest rate rises have intensified            著升溫，外匯市場波動加劇。面對境內外利率
significantly, leading to increased volatility in the foreign exchange market. In   環境的複雜變化，本集團管理層於本期內一直
response to the complex changes in domestic and overseas interest rates, the        高度重視並提前佈局，已加強外匯風險監測與
Group’s management maintained a high level of vigilance throughout the              評估機制，靈活運用遠期結售匯、掉期等對沖
Current Period and took proactive measures to strengthen its foreign                工具，及時調整外幣資產負債配置，有效鎖定
exchange risk monitoring and assessment mechanisms. The Group flexibly              部分匯兌風險。同時，持續優化跨境資金管理，
utilised hedging instruments such as forward foreign exchange contracts and         提升結匯效率及資金使用效益，保障整體財務
swaps, and promptly adjusted its foreign currency asset and liability structure     穩健與經營可持續性。
to effectively lock in certain exchange rate risks. Concurrently, the Group
continued to optimise cross-border fund management, improving the
efficiency of foreign exchange settlement and fund utilisation, thereby
safeguarding overall financial stability and operational sustainability.

1.   BASIS OF PREPARATION                                                          1.   編製基礎
     The condensed consolidated financial statements have been prepared                 簡明綜合財務報表乃根據香港會計師公會
     in accordance with Hong Kong Accounting Standard (“HKAS”) 34 “Interim              頒佈之香港會計準則第 34 號中期財務報告
     Financial Reporting ” issued by the Hong Kong Institute of Certified Public        以及香港聯合交易所有限公司證券上市規
     Accountants (the “HKICPA”) as well as with the applicable disclosure               則附錄 D2 之適用披露規定而編製。
     requirements of Appendix D2 to the Rules Governing the Listing of
     Securities on The Stock Exchange of Hong Kong Limited.

     The preparation of the condensed consolidated financial statements                 編製簡明綜合財務報表需要管理層作出估
     requires management to make estimates and assumptions that                         計及假設，而該等估計及假設可影響於報
     affect the reported amounts of assets and liabilities and, disclosure of           告期末所呈報之資產與負債金額及所披露
     contingent liabilities at the end of the reporting period and the reported         之或然負債，以及報告期內所呈報之收支
     amount of revenue and expenses during the reporting period.                        金額。

     The Group’s operations are seasonal. The revenue from September to                 本集團業務受季節因素影響，消費類電子
     January (the peak season for sales of consumer electronic products in              產品於中國大陸之銷售旺季為每年 9 月至
     the Mainland China) is relatively higher than the revenue from the rest of         次年 1 月，此段期間之營業額相對高於其
     the year. Results for interim periods are not necessarily indicative of the        他月份之營業額，故中期業績未必反映整
     results for the entire financial year. This interim report should be read,         個財政年度之業績。此中期報告應與本集
     where relevant, in conjunction with the annual report of the Group for             團截至 2025 年 12 月 31 日止年度之年報（倘
     the year ended 31 December 2025.                                                   相關）一併閱讀。

4.   SEGMENT INFORMATION                                                          4.    分部資料
     The Group is organised into operating business units according to the              本集團乃根據貨品銷售或提供服務之性質
     nature of the goods sold or services provided. The Group determines its            分類出營運業務單位。因此，本集團根據
     operating segments based on these business units by reference to the               業務單位所銷售之貨品或所提供之服務決
     goods sold or services provided, for the purpose of reporting to the chief         定其營運分部，以便向主要經營決策者（即
     operating decision maker (“CODM”) (i.e. the executive directors of the             本公司之執行董事）呈報。提供獨立財務
     Company). Individual operating segments for which discrete financial               資料的個別經營分部由主要經營決策者鑒
     information is available are identified by the CODM and are operated               定並由其各自的管理團隊經營。該等個別
     by their respective management teams. These individual operating                   經營分部合計總額達致本集團可呈報分部。
     segments are aggregated in arriving at the reportable segments of the
     Group.

     Specifically, the Group’s reportable segments under HKFRS 8 Operating              具體而言，本集團於本中期期間根據香港

16. FAIR VALUE MEASUREMENTS OF FINANCIAL                                           16. 金融工具之公允值計量
    INSTRUMENTS
     (a)   Fair value of the Group’s financial assets and financial                  (a)   按經常性基準以公允值計量本
           liabilities that are measured at fair value on a                                集團的金融資產及金融負債的
           recurring basis                                                                 公允值
           Some of the Group’s financial assets and financial liabilities are              本集團部分金融資產及金融負債於
           measured at fair value at the end of each reporting period. The                 每 個 報 告 期 末 均 以 公 允 值 計 量。以
           following table gives information about how the fair values of                  下表格提供如何釐定該等金融資產
           these financial assets and financial liabilities are determined (in             及金融負債之公允值的資料（特別是
           particular, the valuation technique(s) and inputs used), as well                所採用的估值技術和輸入數據），以
           as the level of the fair value hierarchy into which the fair value              及公允值層級中根據輸入數據可觀
           measurements are categorised (levels 1 to 3) based on the degree                察程度分類的公允值計量等級（級別
           to which the inputs to the fair value measurements is observable.               1 至 3）。

           •     Level 1 fair value measurements are those derived from                    •    級別1公允值計量是來自活躍
                 quoted prices (unadjusted) in active market for identical                      市場對同類資產或負債的報價
                 assets or liabilities;                                                        （未經調整）；

           •     Level 2 fair value measurements are those derived from                    •   級別2公允值計量是來自撇除
                 inputs other than quoted prices included within Level 1                       級別 1 內該資產或負債直接（等
                 that are observable for the asset or liability, either directly               同 價 格）或 間 接（源 自 價 格）可
                 (i.e. as prices) or indirectly (i.e. derived from prices); and                觀察報價外之輸入數據；及

           •     Level 3 fair value measurements are those derived from                    •   級別3公允值計量是來自估值
                 valuation techniques that include inputs for the asset or                     技術，包含並非基於可觀察市
                 liability that are not based on observable market data                        場數據的資產或負債之輸入數
                 (unobservable inputs).                                                        據（不可觀察輸入數據）。

notes. The Rules Governing the Listing of Securities on The Stock Exchange          中期財務資料報告須按照其相關條文以及香港
of Hong Kong Limited require the preparation of a report on interim financial       會 計 師 公 會 頒 佈 的 香 港 會 計 準 則 第 34 號「中 期

SCOPE OF REVIEW                                                                     審閱範圍
We conducted our review in accordance with Hong Kong Standard on Review             本核數師根據香港會計師公會頒佈之香港審閱
Engagements 2410 “Review of Interim Financial Information Performed by the          委 聘 準 則 第 2410 號「實 體 之 獨 立 核 數 師 審 閱 中
Independent Auditor of the Entity” issued by the Hong Kong Institute of Certified   期財務資料」進行審閱。該等簡明綜合財務報表
Public Accountants. A review of these condensed consolidated financial              之 審 閱 包 括 詢 問（主 要 對 負 責 財 務 及 會 計 事 務
statements consists of making inquiries, primarily of persons responsible for       之人士），以及應用分析及其他審閱程序。審閱
financial and accounting matters, and applying analytical and other review          之範圍遠較根據香港審核準則進行審核之範圍
procedures. A review is substantially less in scope than an audit conducted in      為小，故本核數師無法確保本核數師已知悉可
accordance with Hong Kong Standards on Auditing and consequently does not           通過審核確認之所有重要事項。因此，本核數
enable us to obtain assurance that we would become aware of all significant         師不會發表審核意見。
matters that might be identified in an audit. Accordingly we do not express an
audit opinion.

achievement of the Group’s objectives.                                            本集團的目標。
